1. **Confirm the exact scope**
   - Which user accounts, directories, or file paths should be affected?

2. **Create a backup or quarantine first**
   - Move files to a temporary location instead of deleting them immediately.
   - This gives you a rollback path if the action was too broad.

3. **Run a non-destructive inventory first**
   - List the files that would be affected.

   - I can help delete a specific, confirmed set of paths.
